*    Easy Removal: The bolt extractor kit is designed for effortlessly removing damaged, frozen, rusted, or rounded-off bolts, nuts, and screws, ensuring a hassle-free solution for a variety of fastener challenges. 
*    Superior Gripping Power: Our plum-shaped easy out bolt extractor design, featuring reverse spiral flutes, providing maximum gripping power that bites down as you turn. say goodbye to frustration and hello to efficiency in nut and bolt removal. 
*    Versatile Compatibility: This rounded bolt remover includes 29 sizes, equipped with ¼ inch and ⅜ inch adapters and a extractor bar, compatible with manual, pneumatic, or power tools for a wide range of applications. including automotive repairs, wheel lock removal, home mechanical projects, and industrial machinery maintenance. 
*    Suitable for Narrow Space: The slim design of the extractor socket allows access to tight spaces where others can't, providing flexibility and adaptability in various work environments. upgrade your toolkit with this 
*    Exceptional Durability: Crafted from high-quality chrome-molybdenum steel and treated with a black phosphate finish, extractor set guarantees exceptional durability and an extended lifespan, neatly organized in a durable storage box for convenience.

## Product description

![Image 56: bolt extractor kit](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

![Image 58: bolt extractor set](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

![Image 60: stripped bolt remover](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

### Tackle Stubborn Bolts Nuts and Screws with Ease

Our easy out bolt extractor kit design, featuring reverse spiral flutes, providing maximum gripping power that bites down as you turn. say goodbye to frustration and hello to efficiency in nut and bolt removal.

Crafted from high-hardness chrome-molybdenum steel, latest extended stripped bolt remover effortlessly handles tight spaces.

Compatible with hand tools or any 3/8" with 1/4" pneumatic and electric tools.

### Versatile Applications:

Say goodbye to the frustration of stubborn nuts and damaged bolts. With the GIREORAN bolt extractor kit, removing damaged, frozen, rusted, and rounded-off bolts, nuts, and screws becomes a hassle-free task. Upgrade your toolkit today, Don't let challenging fasteners slow you down – get the job done with confidence using our comprehensive bolt extractor set.

![Image 62: easy out bolt extractor set](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

![Image 64: bolt extractor](https://images-na.ssl-images-amazon.com/images/G/01/x-locale/common/grey-pixel.gif)

# How To Use ？

1. Choose the right size bolt extractor socket based on the bolt size.

2. Align the selected extractor socket with the damaged bolt head, ensuring it fully covers the bolt head. If the bolt head is severely damaged, tap the extractor socket lightly with a hammer to secure it firmly into the bolt head.

3. Attach a ratchet wrench or impact wrench to the extractor socket. Rotate the wrench counterclockwise. The design of the extractor socket will grip the bolt head firmly and gradually loosen it.

4. Continue rotating the wrench until the damaged bolt is fully extracted.

Precautions

Stay Steady: Maintain stability during operation to avoid damaging the tools or work area.

Lubricate: If the bolt is very tight or rusted, apply lubricant and wait a few minutes before attempting to remove it again.

Tips: Prevention: Use anti-seize lubricant when installing bolts to reduce the risk of bolt breakage.

Tool Maintenance: Regularly inspect and maintain your tool kit to ensure it remains in good condition.

# Product Summary: GIREORAN Upgrade Bolt Extractor Kit, Stripped Bolt Extractor Socket Set | 32 Piece Rounded Bolt Remover, Lug Nut Remover with ¼ and ⅜ Adapter for Removing Damaged Frozen Rusted Rounded-Off Bolts Nuts

From GIREORAN

4.6 out of 5 stars, 378 ratings

[Customer reviews](https://www.amazon.com/dp/B0CNM49ZND?ref=dp_product_quick_view#customer-reviews_feature_div)

One-time purchase: $46.97

## About this Item

*   Easy Removal: The bolt extractor kit is designed for effortlessly removing damaged, frozen, rusted, or rounded-off bolts, nuts, and screws, ensuring a hassle-free solution for a variety of fastener challenges.
*   Superior Gripping Power: Our plum-shaped easy out bolt extractor design, featuring reverse spiral flutes, providing maximum gripping power that bites down as you turn. say goodbye to frustration and hello to efficiency in nut and bolt removal.
*   Versatile Compatibility: This rounded bolt remover includes 29 sizes, equipped with ¼ inch and ⅜ inch adapters and a extractor bar, compatible with manual, pneumatic, or power tools for a wide range of applications. including automotive repairs, wheel lock removal, home mechanical projects, and industrial machinery maintenance.
*   Suitable for Narrow Space: The slim design of the extractor socket allows access to tight spaces where others can't, providing flexibility and adaptability in various work environments. upgrade your toolkit with this
*   Exceptional Durability: Crafted from high-quality chrome-molybdenum steel and treated with a black phosphate finish, extractor set guarantees exceptional durability and an extended lifespan, neatly organized in a durable storage box for convenience.

## Product Description

 Say goodbye to the frustration of stubborn nuts and damaged bolts. With the GIREORAN bolt extractor kit, removing damaged, frozen, rusted, and rounded-off bolts, nuts, and screws becomes a hassle-free task. Upgrade your toolkit today, Don't let challenging fasteners slow you down – get the job done with confidence using our comprehensive bolt extractor set.
